Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The transfer variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 218 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 955 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_218(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 218. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^955(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 710. QED.

Theorem 213.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the transfer space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 4 and the convergence rate is O(303/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 4 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the transfer structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 303. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
